\begin{array}[]{ll}&V^{\,(1)\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}=-\hskip 0.56917pt\displaystyle\frac{{\mathcal{V}}^{\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}}{\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}}\,\delta({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}),\\[17.22217pt]
&V^{\,(3)\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}=-\hskip 0.56917pt\displaystyle\frac{{\mathcal{U}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt*\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}}{\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}+\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}+\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}}\,\delta({\bf k}+{\bf k}_{1}+{\bf k}_{2}).\end{array}

(4.4)

The coefficient
V^{\,(2)\,a\,a_{1}\,a_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
is found from Eq. (
3.9
). These expressions

Thus, instead of the sum of the initial Hamiltonians
H^{(0)}+H^{(3)}
, Eq. (
4.1
), we now obtain a new free-field Hamiltonian
{\mathcal{H}}^{(0)}
for noninteracting plasmons in terms of the new normal variables
c^{\ast\hskip 0.85355pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
and
c^{\!\!\phantom{\ast}a}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}
:

{\mathcal{H}}^{(0)}=\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,(\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{k}})\ \!c^{\ast\hskip 0.85355pta}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\!\!\phantom{\ast}a}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}.

(4.5)

Hereinafter, the Hamiltonians in the new variables will be designated by the calligraphic letter
{\mathcal{H}}
.

Furthermore, we can move to the construction of an explicit form of effective fourth-order Hamiltonian
{\mathcal{H}}^{(4)}
, which describes the elastic scattering of plasmon off hard particle. For this purpose, we need to collect all contributions proportional to the product
c^{\ast\ \!\!a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}c^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,a}
from the free-field Hamiltonian
H^{(0)}
, Eq. (
2.9
), and from the interaction Hamiltonians
H^{(3)}
and
H^{(4)}
, Eqs. (
2.11
) and (
2.12
), to be generated by the canonical transformations (
3.6
) and (
3.7
). Thereby we obtain the effective fourth-order Hamiltonian describing the elastic scattering process of plasmon off a hard color-charged particle:

{\mathcal{H}}^{(4)}_{g\hskip 0.56917ptG\hskip 0.56917pt\rightarrow\hskip 0.56917ptg\hskip 0.56917ptG}=i\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{2}\hskip 1.13791pt\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}\,c^{\ast\ \!\!a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}c^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{2}}\mathcal{Q}^{\,a},

(4.6)

where the
complete effective amplitude

\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\hskip 0.85355pta\;a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
has the following structure:

i\hskip 0.56917pt\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}=i\hskip 1.13791ptT^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}+\,\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{k}}_{2}\bigr)\,\widetilde{V}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\,a_{2}\hskip 1.13791pta_{1}\,a}_{\ {\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}_{1}}+\bigl(\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{k}}_{1}\bigr)\,\widetilde{V}^{\,(2)\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,a}_{\ {\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}\Bigr]

(4.7)

+\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,(\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot{\mathbf{k}})\,\Bigl\{F^{\,\ast}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptV^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}+F^{\phantom{\ast}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptV^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\,a\,a_{2}\,a_{1}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\Bigr\}

+\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,\Bigl\{\upphi^{\phantom{\ast}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptV^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}+\hskip 0.85355pt\upphi^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\bf k}\,V^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\,a\,a_{2}\,a_{1}}_{\ {\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\Bigr\}

+\,\Bigl[\upphi^{\phantom{\ast}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.56917ptM^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,a_{1}\,a}_{\;{\bf k}_{1}}\,+\,\upphi^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436ptM^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,a}_{\;{\bf k}_{2}}\Bigr]

+\,2\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}\,\Bigl\{\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{V}}^{\;a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,a}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}}\,F^{\phantom{\ast}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\bf k}\hskip 1.13791pt\delta({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}-{\bf k})\,+\,{\mathcal{V}}^{\,*\,a_{2}\,a_{1}\,a}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}}\,F^{\,\ast}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\bf k}\hskip 1.13791pt\delta({\bf k}_{2}-{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k})\Bigr\}.

Note that the complete effective amplitude
\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
must satisfy the following symmetry condition

\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}=-\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\,a\,a_{2}\,a_{1}}_{\;{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}_{1}}.

(4.8)

This relation is a consequence of the requirement of reality for the effective Hamiltonian (
4.6
). We already faced this condition for the vertex function
T^{\,(2)\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\,{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}
in section
2
.

Let us analyze the structure
\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
in more detail. The first step is to use the relation (
3.12
) to get rid of the coefficient function
\widetilde{V}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\,a_{2}\,a_{1}\,a}_{\ {\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}_{1}}
in the first line of (
4.7
). Further, note that the expressions in the second and third lines are mutually reduced with the use of (
4.2
). For the coefficient function
M^{\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1}}
in the next fourth line we make use of its connection with the coefficient function
F^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}_{\,{\bf k}_{1}}
, Eq. (
3.10
). Finally, in the last contribution in (
4.7
), with the vertex function
{\mathcal{V}}^{\;a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,a}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}}
, we perform an integration with respect to
{\bf k}
, take into account color decomposition (
2.14
) and pass from the coefficient function
F^{\phantom{\ast}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt\bf k}
to the ‘‘physical’’ vertex function
\upphi^{\phantom{\ast}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}
by the rule (
4.2
). As a result, instead of (
4.7
), we finally obtain

\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}=-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 1.13791pt\bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\mathbf{k}}_{1}-{\mathbf{k}}_{2})\bigr]\,\widetilde{V}^{\,(2)\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,a}_{\ {\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}+\,\widetilde{T}^{\,(2)\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,a}_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}},

(4.9)

where the effective amplitude
\widetilde{T}^{\,(2)\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,a}_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
has the following structure:

\widetilde{T}^{\,(2)\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,a}_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}=T^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}

(4.10)

+\,f^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,a}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\frac{{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast}_{\,{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt{\upphi}^{\phantom{\ast}}_{\,{\bf k}_{2}}}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}-{\bf v}\cdot{\bf k}_{2}}\,+\,2\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.56917pt\biggl(\,\frac{{\mathcal{V}}^{\phantom{\ast}}_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}}{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt{\ast}}_{\,{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}}}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}}\!-{\bf v}\cdot({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2})}\,-\,\frac{{\mathcal{V}}^{\,{\ast}}_{\,{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}-{\bf k}_{1}}{\upphi}^{\phantom{\ast}}_{\,{\bf k}_{2}-{\bf k}_{1}}}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}-{\bf k}_{1}}\!-{\bf v}\cdot({\bf k}_{2}-{\bf k}_{1})}\biggr)\!\biggr\}.

We can simplify this amplitude somewhat more by taking into account the conjugation property for the three-plasmon vertex:

{\mathcal{V}}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}_{\,{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}-{\bf k}_{1}}={\mathcal{V}}_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}},

but we won’t do it. The first term on the right-hand side of (
4.9
) has the resonance factor
\bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\mathbf{k}}_{1}-{\mathbf{k}}_{2})\bigr]
, which in fact represents a consequence of the momentum and energy conservation laws in the scattering process under investigation. If this resonance condition is approximately satisfied, then the contribution of this term to the effective Hamiltonian can be completely neglected. In section
12
we will discuss in more detail the case when the ‘‘resonance frequency difference’’

\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathbf{k}}_{1},\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathbf{k}}_{2}}\equiv\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathbf{k}}_{2}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathbf{k}}_{1}-{\mathbf{k}}_{2})

(4.11)

can be arbitrary and not necessarily small.

Figure
2
gives the diagrammatic interpretation of different terms in curly brackets in the effective amplitude (
4.10
).

Figure 2:
The effective amplitude
\widetilde{T}^{\,(2)\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,a}_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
for the elastic scattering process of plasmon off a hard color particle. The blob stands for HTL resummation and the double line denotes the hard particle

The first two graphs represent the Compton scattering of soft boson excitations off a hard test particle induced by the first term in curly brackets of the expression (
4.10
). The incoming and outgoing wave lines in fig.
2
correspond
to the normal variables
c^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}
and
c^{\ast\ \!\!a_{2}}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}
, respectively, and the horizontal double line between two interaction vertices corresponds to the ‘‘propagator’’ of the hard particle

\frac{\!\!1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}-{\bf v}\cdot{\bf k}_{1}}.

(4.12)

The interaction vertices correspond to the functions
{\upphi}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\ast}_{\,{\bf k}_{1}}
or
{\upphi}^{\phantom{\ast}}_{\,{\bf k}_{2}}
. The remaining graph is connected with the interaction of hard particle with plasmons through the three-plasmon vertex function
{\mathcal{V}}^{\;a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
with intermediate ‘‘virtual’’ oscillation to which the factor

\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}}\!-{\bf v}\cdot({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2})}

in (
4.10
) corresponds.
Note that this factor can also be written in a slightly different form

\frac{1}{\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2}}\!-\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}+\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}},

if the resonance frequency difference (
4.11
) is exactly zero. The last expression represents (up to a multiplier) an approximation of the effective gluon propagator
{}^{\ast}\widetilde{\cal D}_{\mu\hskip 0.56917pt\nu}(k)
, Eqs. (
A.7
)–(
A.10
), at the plasmon
pole
\omega\sim\omega^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}
in a spirit of the works by Weldon
[
58
]
and Blaizot and Iancu
[
55
]
. We considered such an approximation in
[
1
,
2
]
.

Finally, the first term
T^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
on the right-hand side of (
4.10
) should be associated with the process of direct interaction of two plasmons with a hard particle, as depicted in fig.
3
.

Figure 3:
Direct interaction of two plasmons with a hard particle

In the particular physical system under consideration there is no a double contact vertex function that would describe this scattering process
7
7

7

However, one may recall here the second order formalism for fermions
[
59
,
60
,
61
,
62
]
, within which the interaction vertex of similar type arises. It is not clear whether such a vertex can be defined within our purely classical formalism, without fermions. Besides it is possible to mention a double photon vertex function in the theory of a complex (pseudo)scalar field
\phi
interacting with the electromagnetic field
A_{\mu}

[
63
,
64
]
(scalar QED). With a minimal generalization to color degrees of freedom (scalar QCD) perhaps this will give us a nontrivial representation for the
T^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
- vertex.
and therefore we should just assume that

T^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}\equiv 0.

Taking into account the last circumstance and
substituting the vertex function
{\upphi}^{\phantom{\ast}}_{\,{\bf k}}
, Eq. (
2.18
), into (
4.10
), we find the final form for the effective amplitude
\hskip 0.85355pt\widetilde{T}^{\,(2)\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,a}_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
. The expression thus obtained coincides with the effective amplitude derived in the framework of high-temperature chromodynamics in
[
65
]
.

5
Fourth-order correlation function

Now we turn to the construction of a kinetic equation describing the elastic scattering process of a plasmon off a hard color particle. As the free Hamiltonian here, we take the new free Hamiltonian
{\mathcal{H}}^{(0)}
, Eq. (
4.5
), and as the interaction Hamiltonian we consider the effective Hamiltonian
{\mathcal{H}}^{(4)}_{g\hskip 0.56917ptG\hskip 0.56917pt\rightarrow\hskip 0.56917ptg\hskip 0.56917ptG}
, Eq. (
4.6
). The equations of motion for the bosonic normal variables
c^{\phantom{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}\!\!a^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}
and
c^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,a}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
and the color charge
\mathcal{Q}^{\,a}
are defined by the corresponding Hamilton equations. For soft Bose-excitations we find

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\phantom{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}\!\!a^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}}{\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 1.42271pt\Bigl\{c^{\phantom{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}\!\!a^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{H}}^{(0)\!}+{\mathcal{H}}^{(4)}_{g\hskip 0.56917ptG\hskip 0.56917pt\rightarrow\hskip 0.56917ptg\hskip 0.56917ptG}\Bigr\}=-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}-{\bf v}\cdot{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\bigr)\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\phantom{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}\!\!a^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}+\!\int\!d\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,a^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd}_{\;{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\,c^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt,

(5.1)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,a}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}}{\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 1.42271pt\Bigl\{c^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,a}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{H}}^{(0)\!}+{\mathcal{H}}^{(4)}_{g\hskip 0.56917ptG\hskip 0.56917pt\rightarrow\hskip 0.56917ptg\hskip 0.56917ptG}\Bigr\}=i\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}-{\bf v}\cdot{\bf k}\bigr)\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,a}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}-\!\int\!d\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,\ast\hskip 1.13791pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\,c^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt,

(5.2)

and, respectively, for the classical color charge we get

\frac{d\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=\Bigl\{\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt,\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{H}}^{(0)\!}+{\mathcal{H}}^{(4)}_{g\hskip 0.56917ptG\hskip 0.56917pt\rightarrow\hskip 0.56917ptg\hskip 0.56917ptG}\Bigr\}=\frac{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{H}}^{(0)\!}+{\mathcal{H}}^{(4)}_{g\hskip 0.56917ptG\hskip 0.56917pt\rightarrow\hskip 0.56917ptg\hskip 0.56917ptG}\bigr)}{\,\partial\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}}\,f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}

(5.3)

=i\hskip 0.56917ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}\,\mathscr{T}^{\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,d^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}c^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt.

Here and hereafter, for the sake of simplicity, we have omitted the tag ‘‘(2)’’ in the designation of the complete effective amplitude
\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt(2)\hskip 0.85355pta\;a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
. If the ensemble of interacting Bose-excitations at a low nonlinearity level has random phases, then it can be statistically described by introducing the bosonic correlation function of the following form:

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!a^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle={\mathcal{N}}^{\;a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\delta({\bf k}-{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}),

(5.4)

where
{\mathcal{N}}^{\;a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
is the plasmon number density.

Let us define the kinetic equation for the plasmon number density employing the Hamilton equations (
5.1
) and (
5.2
), and the definition (
5.4
). Using precisely the same reasoning as in
[
1
,
2
]
, we get

\delta({\bf k}-{\bf k}\!\ ^{\prime})\,\frac{\partial\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{N}}^{\;a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}}{\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl\{\mathscr{T}^{\,\ast\,a\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!a^{\prime}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+\mathscr{T}^{\,a^{\prime}\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd}_{\,{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a_{\phantom{1}}\!\!\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}.

(5.5)

Further, by differentiating the fourth-order correlation function
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a_{\phantom{1}}\!\!\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
with respect to
t
with allowance made for (
5.1
) – (
5.3
), we derive the equation the right-hand side of which will contain the fourth- and sixth-order correlation functions in the variables
c^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,a}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}},\,c^{\phantom{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}\!\!a}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
and
\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}

\frac{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a_{\phantom{1}}\!\!\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.56917pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle}{\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=i\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl[\,\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}-{\bf v}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1})\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr]\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a_{\phantom{1}}\!\!\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.56917pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

(5.6)

+\int\!d\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl\{\mathscr{T}^{\,\ast\,a\,a^{\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pte}_{\,{\bf k},\,{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{1}}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a^{\prime}_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,+\,\mathscr{T}^{\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pte}_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{1}}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a^{\phantom{\prime}}_{\phantom{1}}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\phantom{\prime}}_{\phantom{1}}}\!\!c^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!a^{\prime}_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr\}

+\;i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{2}\,\mathscr{T}^{\,a^{\prime}_{1}\,a^{\prime}_{2}\,d^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}_{\,{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{1},\,{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle c^{\ast\ \!\!a^{\phantom{\prime}}_{\phantom{1}}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\phantom{\prime}}_{\phantom{1}}}\!\!c^{\ \!\!a^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1}}c^{\ast\ \!\!a^{\prime}_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{1}}\,c^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{2}}\,\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}\bigr\rangle.

We close the chain of equations by expressing the sixth-order correlation functions in terms of the pair correlation functions for the normal variables
c^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,a}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
and
c^{\phantom{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast}\!\!a}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
, and the mean value of the color charge
\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}
. We keep only the terms that will give the proper contributions to the required kinetic equation:

\begin{split}&\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a^{\prime}_{1}}_{{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!a^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1}}_{{\bf k}^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1}}\hskip 0.56917pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.56917pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,\simeq\,\delta({\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{1}-{\bf k}^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1})\,{\mathcal{N}}^{\;a^{\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,\\[6.45831pt]
&\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a^{\phantom{\prime}}_{\phantom{1}}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\phantom{\prime}}_{\phantom{1}}}\!\!c^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!a^{\prime}_{1}}_{{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.56917pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.56917pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\simeq\,\delta({\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{1}-{\bf k}^{\phantom{\prime}})\,{\mathcal{N}}^{\;a^{\phantom{\prime}}\!a^{\prime}_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,\\[6.45831pt]
&\bigl\langle c^{\ast\ \!\!a^{\phantom{\prime}}_{\phantom{1}}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\phantom{\prime}}_{\phantom{1}}}\!\!c^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!a^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1}}_{{\bf k}^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1}}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a^{\prime}_{1}}_{{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{1}}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{2}}_{{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{2}}\hskip 0.56917pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}\bigr\rangle\simeq\delta({\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{2}-{\bf k})\hskip 0.85355pt\delta({\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}_{1}-{\bf k}^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1})\,{\mathcal{N}}^{\;a^{\phantom{\prime}}\!a^{\prime}_{2}}_{{\bf k}^{\phantom{\prime}}}{\mathcal{N}}^{\;a^{\prime}_{1}\hskip 0.56917pta^{\phantom{\prime}}_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.\end{split}

(5.7)

We set for the complete effective amplitude
\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
the following color and momentum decomposition

\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}=f^{\hskip 0.85355pta\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\hskip 0.85355pt}\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\phantom{a}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}.

(5.8)

It should be mentioned that the function
\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\phantom{a}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}
, due to the requirement (
4.8
), must meet the condition

\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\phantom{a}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}=\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\phantom{a}}_{\;{\bf k}_{2},\,{\bf k}_{1}}.

(5.9)

Substituting the expressions (
5.7
) and (
5.8
) into the right-hand side of (
5.6
) and considering the symmetry condition (
5.9
) for the scattering amplitude, instead of (
5.6
), we derive the equation for the fourth-order correlation function

\frac{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a_{\phantom{1}}\!\!\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.56917pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle}{\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}\,=\,i\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl[\,\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}-{\bf v}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1})\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr]\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a_{\phantom{1}}\!\!\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917ptc^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.56917pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

(5.10)

+\;i\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,\phantom{a}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\bigl[\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}-\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\,T^{\,e}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}\bigr]\hskip 0.85355pt\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\vskip 5.69046pt\,+\,\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,\phantom{a}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\!}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte}.

Here,
(\hskip 0.28436ptT^{\,a})^{\hskip 0.28436ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}\equiv-i\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.56917ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}
are generators in the adjoint representation and we have introduced a matrix notation for the plasmon number density
{\mathcal{N}}^{\phantom{a_{1}}\!\!\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}=\bigl({\mathcal{N}}^{\,a\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}_{{\bf k}^{\phantom{\prime}}}\bigr)
.

6
Kinetic equation for soft gluon excitations

The self-consistent system of equations (
5.5
) and (
5.10
), when it is supplemented by an equation for the averaged color charge (see section
9
) determines, in principle, the time evolution of the plasmon number density
{\mathcal{N}}^{\;a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
. However, one more simplification is introduced: in
Eq. (
5.10
) we disregard the term with a time derivative compared to the term containing the resonance factor
\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\,{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}
, Eq. (
4.11
). Instead of equation (
5.10
), we now have

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a_{\phantom{1}}\!\!\!}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!a_{1}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.56917pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\simeq\delta({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1})\,{\mathcal{N}}^{\;a\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\vskip 4.26773pt

(6.1)

-\;\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\,{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}\!+i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,\phantom{a}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\bigl[\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}-\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\,T^{\,e}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}\bigr]\hskip 0.85355pt\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

-\,\frac{i}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\,{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}\!+i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,\phantom{a}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\,T^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\!}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte}.

Here, the first term on the right-hand side, which corresponds to the absence of interaction between soft gluon excitations and a hard color particle is the solution to the homogeneous equation for the fourth-order correlation function

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a_{\phantom{1}}\!\!\!}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!a_{1}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
. The other terms determine the deviation of the three-point correlator from the free interaction case.

We substitute the first term from (
6.1
) into the right-hand side of Eq. (
5.5
) for
{\mathcal{N}}^{\;a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
. As a result we obtain

-\hskip 0.85355pti\,\Bigl\{\mathscr{T}^{\,\phantom{a}}_{\;{\bf k}^{\prime},\,{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\,T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}-\,\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,\phantom{a}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf k}^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}^{\prime}}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\Bigr\}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

(6.2)

Further, we substitute the second and third terms from (
6.1
) into the right-hand side of Eq. (
5.5
). Simple algebraic transformations, in view of the symmetry condition (
5.9
), lead us to

-\hskip 0.28436pt\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}\biggl(\mathscr{T}^{\ast}_{\,{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\,\mathscr{T}^{\phantom{\ast}}_{\,{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\!,\,{\bf k}_{1}}\times

(6.3)

\displaystyle\times\biggl\{

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}\!+\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.85355pt0}\Bigl(\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\!{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\!f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\prime}\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+i\hskip 1.13791pt\Bigl[\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\!-\,\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\Bigr]\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr)\vskip 3.41418pt

\displaystyle-\,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}\!-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.85355pt0}\Bigl(\bigl(T^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\!{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\!f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+i\hskip 1.13791pt\Bigl[\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\!-\bigl(T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\Bigr]\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr)\!\biggr\}.

In the second term in braces, by virtue of the definition of (
4.11
), we took into account that

\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}+\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.85355pt0}=-\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}\!-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.85355pt0}.

Considering the expressions obtained (
5.5
), (
6.2
), and (
6.3
) and setting
{\bf k}={\bf k}^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}
, we arrive at the following kinetic equation for the plasmon number density
{\mathcal{N}}^{\;a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
, instead of (
5.5
):

\frac{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}^{\;a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}}{\!\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=-\hskip 0.85355pti\,\Bigl\{\mathscr{T}^{\,\phantom{a}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\,T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}-\,\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,\phantom{a}}_{\;{\bf k},\,{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\Bigr\}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle-\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}\,\bigl|\mathscr{T}_{{\bf k},\,{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,\times\vskip-7.11317pt

(6.4)

\displaystyle\times\hskip 0.56917pt\biggl\{

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 1.13791pt{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}\!+\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.85355pt0}\Bigl(\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 1.13791ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\!\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\!f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+i\hskip 1.13791pt\Bigl[\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 1.13791ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\!-\,\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\Bigr]\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr)\vskip 3.41418pt

\displaystyle-\,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 1.13791pt{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}\!-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.85355pt0}\Bigl(\bigl(T^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\!}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\!f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle+i\hskip 1.13791pt\Bigl[\bigl(T^{\,e}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\!-\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}}\Bigr]\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr)\!\biggr\}.

As opposed to our previous works
[
1
,
2
]
, we literally do not gather the difference we need in the resulting expression

\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 1.13791pt{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}\!+i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,-\,\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 1.13791pt{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}\!-i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\;\big(\!\equiv-\hskip 0.85355pt2\hskip 0.56917pti\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\Delta\hskip 0.85355pt\omega_{\hskip 1.13791pt{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}})\bigr),

(6.5)

since here we have different arrangements of the plasmon number density matrices with different wave vectors:
{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}
and
{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}
.

7
First moment about color of the kinetic equation (
6.4
)

Let us consider the following color decomposition of the matrix function
{\mathcal{N}}^{\;a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}
:

{\mathcal{N}}^{\;a\hskip 0.85355pta^{\prime}_{\phantom{1}}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}=\delta^{\,a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}\!\hskip 0.28436ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}+\bigl(T^{\,c}\bigr)^{a\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}}\!\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}.

(7.1)

In this section we define an equation for the colorless part of the plasmon number density, i.e. for
N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}
. For this purpose, we take the trace of the left- and right-hand sides of equation (
6.4
) with respect to the color indices, i.e. we set
a=a^{\prime}
and perform the summation over
a
. Using the color expansion (
7.1
) and the formulae for the traces of the product of two and three color matrices (generators) in the adjoint representation in Appendix
D
, Eqs. (
D.1
) and (
D.2
), we easily find for terms linear in the matrix functions
{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}
and
{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}
on the right-hand side of (
6.4
)

{\rm tr}\,{\mathcal{N}}_{\bf k}=(N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-1)N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\qquad{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)=N_{c}\hskip 1.13791pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k},

(7.2)

{\rm tr}\bigl[\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigr]=\delta^{e\hskip 0.85355ptd}N_{c}\bigl(N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}-N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)-\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\bigl(T^{\,c}\bigr)^{e\hskip 0.85355ptd}\bigl(\hskip 0.56917ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}+W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle,

{\rm tr}\bigl[\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)-\bigl(T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigr]=\delta^{e\hskip 0.85355ptd}N_{c}\bigl(N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}-N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)-\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\bigl(T^{\,c}\bigr)^{e\hskip 0.85355ptd}\bigl(\hskip 0.56917ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}+W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

In contracting the last two expressions with
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
, the terms with a sum
W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}+W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}
on the right-hand sides turn to zero.

The traces of terms quadratic in
{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}
and
{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}
in (
6.4
) have a slightly more complicated structure. Thus, for example,

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 1.13791ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\!\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=N_{c}\hskip 1.13791pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}d}\hskip 0.56917ptN_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{{\bf k}_{1}}

(7.3)

+\,\frac{i}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}d\,c\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{{\bf k}_{1}}-\frac{i}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}d\,c\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436ptW_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptN_{{\bf k}}+{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}T^{\,f}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptW_{{\bf k}_{1}}.

If we further contract this expression with the antisymmetric structure constants
f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}e}
, as it takes place in (
6.4
), then the first term on the right-hand side of (
7.3
) turns to zero. The last term also vanishes due to the symmetry property for the trace of the product of four generators (
D.6
). As a result, we have

f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 1.13791ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\!\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{i}{2}\,N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl(W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr).

With the expressions obtained for the color traces, we can now write out the first moment about color for equation (
6.4
)

d_{A}\hskip 1.13791pt\frac{\partial\hskip 0.28436ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl({\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}_{{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}\bigr)\hskip 0.28436ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}

(7.4)

-\hskip 0.85355ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 1.13791pt\bigl|\mathscr{T}_{{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt\Bigl\{\bigl(N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}}-N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)-\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)\!\Bigr\}\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}\!-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1})).

In deriving this expression, we have considered Sokhotsky’s formula (
6.5
) and introduced the notation for a colorless quadratic combination of the averaged color charge

{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}(t)\equiv\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

(7.5)

The coefficient
d_{A}\equiv N^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}_{c}-1
on the left-hand side of (
7.4
) is an invariant for the group
SU(N_{c})
.

8
Second moment about color of the kinetic equation (
6.4
)

Let us return to the matrix kinetic equation (
6.4
). We now contract the left- and right-hand sides of this equation with the color matrix
(\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s})^{\hskip 0.56917pta^{\prime}\hskip 0.28436pta}
. As a result we get

\frac{\partial\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=-i\,\Bigl\{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}_{{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}T^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,\ast}_{{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}\Bigr\}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

(8.1)

-\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 1.13791pt\bigl|\mathscr{T}_{{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\,\times\vskip-4.26773pt

\displaystyle\times\hskip 0.56917pt\biggl\{

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}\!+\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.85355pt0}\Bigl({\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\!{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte\hskip 0.85355pt}+i\hskip 1.13791pt\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\!-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\Bigr]\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\vskip 3.41418pt

\displaystyle-\,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}\!-\hskip 0.85355pti\hskip 0.85355pt0}\Bigl({\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436ptT^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte\hskip 0.85355pt}+i\hskip 1.13791pt\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\!-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\Bigr]\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\Bigr)\!\biggr\}.

Here on the left-hand side, by virtue of the decomposition (
7.1
) we have again

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)=N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}.

(8.2)

Let us consider the traces in the first term on the right-hand side of (
8.1
). By taking into account the representation (
7.1
), a simple calculation leads us to

{\rm tr}\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917pts}N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}+\frac{i}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\,e\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k},\quad{\rm tr}\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917pts}N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}-\frac{i}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\,e\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}.

The imaginary part in these two expressions turns to zero by contracting with the color charge

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
and finally the first term on the right-hand side (
8.1
) takes the following form:

-\hskip 0.85355pti\,\Bigl\{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,e}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}^{\,\ast}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}\Bigr\}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=2\hskip 0.56917ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}\bigr)\hskip 0.28436ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

(8.3)

We now examine the traces of terms quadratic in
{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}
and
{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}
in (
8.1
). Allowing for the decomposition (
7.1
), we easily find for the first trace there

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\!{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.99594ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)N_{{\bf k}}N_{{\bf k}_{1}}+{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle W_{{\bf k}}N_{{\bf k}_{1}}

(8.4)

+\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle N_{{\bf k}}W_{{\bf k}_{1}}+{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}T^{\,f}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle W_{{\bf k}}W_{{\bf k}_{1}}.

Let us contract this expression with
f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}e\hskip 0.56917pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
, as it takes place in (
8.1
). For the first term on the right-hand side of (
8.4
), by virtue of formula (
D.2
), we have

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)N_{{\bf k}}N_{{\bf k}_{1}}f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{i}{2}\,N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle N_{{\bf k}}N_{{\bf k}_{1}}.

The third term in (
8.4
), containing the trace of the product of four generators turns to zero due to the symmetry property (
D.6
). The trace in the second term in (
8.4
) is easily computed as follows

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte\hskip 0.85355pt}

\equiv\frac{1}{2}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl[\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\!,T^{\,d}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr]T^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte}=\frac{i}{2}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917pt\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,\lambda}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}d\,\lambda\hskip 0.85355pt}f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte}=-\!\left(\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{i}{2}\hskip 0.85355pt\right)^{\!\!2}\!N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\,e\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355pt}.

When constructing this expression with
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
it turns to zero. Thus, instead of (
8.4
), we now have

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\!{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.99594ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigr)f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.28436pte}\bigr\rangle

(8.5)

=-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{i}{2}\,N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptN_{{\bf k}}N_{{\bf k}_{1}}+\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}T^{\,f}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW_{{\bf k}}W_{{\bf k}_{1}}.

We still need to consider the remaining trace of the fifth order. Due to the presence of the factor
\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle
, which is symmetric with respect to the indices
c
and
f
, here it is easiest to use the relation (
D.8
) from Appendix
D
. Using this relation, we immediately get

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}T^{\,f}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{i}{2}\,f^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 1.13791ptb}\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,b}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,f}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}T^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

For the fourth-order trace on the right part, we apply formula (
D.3
). Then, after simple algebraic transformations, the right-hand side of the previous expression can be reduced to the following form:

-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{i}{2}\,f^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 1.13791ptb\hskip 1.13791pt}{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,b}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,f}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}T^{\,c}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

(8.6)

=-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{i}{2}\,\biggl\{\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptf\hskip 0.56917ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355pte}-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\Bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,f}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)-{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.28436ptD^{\,c}\hskip 0.28436ptD^{\,f}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)\Bigr]\!\biggr\}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{i}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\biggl\{\hskip 0.28436pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptf\hskip 0.56917ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355pte}-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{2}\,\Bigl(\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.85355ptc}+\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pte\hskip 0.56917ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptf\hskip 0.85355pts}\Bigr)\!\biggr\}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=0.

Here we have used the formulas for the fourth-order traces (
D.3
) and (
D.4
). Thus the coefficient of the product
W_{{\bf k}}W_{{\bf k}_{1}}
on the right-hand side of (
8.5
) is zero and this contribution falls out of consideration.

For the trace
{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)
in (
8.1
), we obtain similar result. In summary, for the quadratic in
{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}
and
{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}
terms in (
8.1
), using the Sohotsky formula (
6.5
) we get

\left\{\frac{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.28436pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\!\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}+\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,-\,\frac{{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}-\,i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\right\}\!f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

(8.7)

=\frac{1}{2}\,i\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}N_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917ptN_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{l}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}-\omega^{l}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1})).

Let us consider the remaining traces or, more precisely, differences of the traces that are linear in
{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}
and
{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}
in square brackets in (
8.1
). Taking into account (
7.1
) for the first difference of the traces, we find

\Bigl[{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\!-\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\Bigr]\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle={\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)\bigl(N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}-N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\!\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

+\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.56917ptT^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,s}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,f}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr)\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}-W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\!\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\vskip 2.84544pt

=\Bigl\{2\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.56917ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.85355pte}+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 1.13791ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.28436ptd^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\Bigr\}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}-W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\!\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

Here we again used the formulae for the traces (
D.2
) and (
D.3
). The term with the difference
N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}-N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}
has turned to zero. The coefficient of the survive term with the difference
W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}-W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}
contains the contraction of two symmetric structure constants, which in the general case of arbitrary
N_{c}
cannot be reduced to an expansion in terms of the Kronecker deltas.

Taking into account the above obtained expression and (
8.2
), (
8.3
), and (
8.7
), instead of (
8.1
) we find a kinetic equation for the second ‘‘color’’ part of the plasmon number density of soft gluon plasma excitations
W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}

N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{\partial\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.56917ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)}{\!\!\partial\hskip 0.85355ptt}=2\hskip 0.42677ptN_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\rm Im}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathscr{T}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}\bigr)\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{\bf k}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

(8.8)

-\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}\,\bigl|\mathscr{T}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\hskip 1.13791pt\Bigl\{2\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917pts\hskip 0.56917ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.56917ptc\hskip 0.85355pte}+\frac{1}{4}\,N_{c}\hskip 1.42271ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pts\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\hskip 0.28436ptd^{\hskip 0.85355ptc\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355pt\lambda}\Bigr\}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}-W^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

\times\,(2\pi)\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1}))

+\;\frac{1}{2}\,N^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 1.13791pt\bigl|\mathscr{T}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,N^{\,l}_{{\bf k}}\hskip 0.56917ptN^{\,l}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pts}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt(2\pi)\,\delta(\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}}-\omega^{\hskip 0.56917ptl}_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}-{\mathbf{v}}\cdot({\bf k}-{\bf k}_{1})).

We can simplify the contraction of two symmetric structure constants in (
8.8
) in two cases. The first ‘‘trivial’’ case relates to
N_{c}=2
, when
d^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}\equiv 0
. The second case refers to
N_{c}=3
and is connected with the availability of a well-known accidental cancellation expressed by the relation (
D.9
) from Appendix
D
. We will discuss this point in more detail in section
10
.

9
Equation for the expected value of color charge
{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}

In this section we consider the derivation of the equation of motion for the expected value of color charge
{\mathcal{Q}}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}
. Our first step is to average equation (
5.3
). Thus, we obtain

\frac{d\hskip 0.28436pt\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=i\hskip 0.56917ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{2}\,\mathscr{T}^{\,a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}\,d^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}\bigl\langle c^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,a_{1}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}c^{\hskip 0.85355pta_{2}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle,

(9.1)

where the fourth-order correlation function on the right-hand side, due to (
6.1
), has the following form

\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355ptc^{\ast\ \!\!a_{1}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436ptc^{\phantom{\ast}\!\!a_{2}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.56917pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\simeq\hskip 0.85355pt\delta({\bf k}_{1}-{\bf k}_{2})\,{\mathcal{N}}^{\;a_{1}\hskip 0.85355pta^{\phantom{\prime}}_{2}\!}_{\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}}\,\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\vskip 4.26773pt

(9.2)

+\;\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}\!+i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,\phantom{a}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}\bigl[\hskip 0.56917pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\prime}}\bigr)^{\,a_{1}\hskip 0.56917pta_{2}}-\bigl(T^{\,e^{\prime}}\!\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)^{a_{1}\hskip 0.56917pta_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr]\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle

-\;\frac{i}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}\!+i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\mathscr{T}^{\hskip 0.85355pt\ast\,\phantom{a}}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl({\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime\prime}\!}{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)^{a_{1}\hskip 0.56917pta_{2}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.56917ptf^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte^{\prime}}.

Let us substitute the first term of the correlation function into the right-hand side of (
9.1
). Then, by taking into account the color factorization of the total effective amplitude, Eq. (
5.8
), we find

i\hskip 0.56917ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{2}\hskip 1.13791pt\mathscr{T}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 1.13791pt{\rm tr}\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}\!\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt.

By virtue of the equality
{\rm tr}\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\!\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigr)=N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\hskip 0.56917pt\bigr\rangle
, the expression above is zero. Furthermore, we substitute the second term from the correlation function (
9.2
) into (
9.1
)

f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{2}\,|\mathscr{T}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}\!+i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,\bigl[{\rm tr}\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\prime}}\!\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,e^{\prime}}\bigr)-{\rm tr}\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}T^{\,e^{\prime}}\!\hskip 0.56917pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)\bigr]\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

Using the corresponding formula for the traces in (
7.2
) for the last expression we easily find

f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}e}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{2}\,|\mathscr{T}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}\!+i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,

\times\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl[\hskip 0.85355pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}e^{\prime}\!}N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}-N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)+\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\bigl(T^{\,c}\bigr)^{d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.56917pte^{\hskip 0.28436pt\prime}\!}\bigl(W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}+W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigr]\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

By virtue of the antisymmetry of the structural constant
f^{\hskip 0.85355pta\hskip 0.85355ptb\hskip 0.85355ptc}
this contribution also turns to zero.

As the last step, we substitute the third term of the correlation function (
9.2
) into the right-hand side of (
9.1
). As a result, we get

-\hskip 0.85355ptif^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte}f^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime\prime}e^{\prime}}\!\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{2}\,|\mathscr{T}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}\!+i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,{\rm tr}\bigl(\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}}\!{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}T^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime\prime}}\!{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigr)\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355pte^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

According to formula (
7.3
) for the trace in the integrand we have

{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(\hskip 0.85355pt{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\!{\mathcal{N}}_{{\bf k}_{2}}T^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)=N_{c}\hskip 1.13791pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime\prime}}\!\hskip 0.71114ptN^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}

(9.3)

+\,\frac{i}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime\prime}\hskip 0.28436ptc\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}-\frac{i}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime\prime}\hskip 0.28436ptc\hskip 0.85355pt}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 1.13791pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}+{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}T^{\,f}T^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.85355ptW^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}W^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}.

For the first term on the right-hand side, we have the following color factor

f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte}f^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime\prime}e^{\prime}}\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime\prime}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=-N_{c}\hskip 1.13791pt\delta^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=-N_{c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

Next, the color factor for the second term in (
9.3
) is

f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte}f^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime\prime}e^{\prime}}\!f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436ptd^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime\prime}\hskip 0.28436ptc\hskip 0.85355pt}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=i\,{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,e^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}T^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle=-\hskip 0.85355pt\frac{1}{2}\,N_{c}\hskip 0.85355ptf^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355pte^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436ptc}\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\equiv 0.

Thus, the contribution with the product
W_{{\bf k}_{1}}\hskip 0.28436ptN_{{\bf k}_{2}}
in (
9.3
) equals zero.
By arguments that are completely analogous to the previous ones, the contribution with the product
W_{{\bf k}_{2}}\hskip 0.28436ptN_{{\bf k}_{1}}
vanishes. Finally, the color factor for the last term (
9.3
) has the form

f^{\hskip 0.85355ptd\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte}f^{\hskip 0.85355pte\hskip 0.85355ptd^{\hskip 0.56917pt\prime\prime}\hskip 0.28436pte^{\prime}}{\rm tr}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigl(T^{\,c}\hskip 0.85355ptT^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime}}T^{\,f}T^{\,d^{\hskip 0.85355pt\prime\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr)\hskip 0.28436pt\bigl\langle\hskip 0.85355pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,e^{\prime}}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptc}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptf}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

This contribution up to a multiplier and redefinition of the color indices coincides with the expression (
8.6
) of the previous section. There we have shown that it is zero and thus the contribution proportional to
W_{{\bf k}_{1}}W_{{\bf k}_{2}}
also drops out and, thus, only the first term survives from the whole expression (
9.3
). As a result, for equation (
9.1
) we have

\frac{d\hskip 0.28436pt\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\,d}\hskip 0.85355pt\rangle}{d\hskip 0.85355ptt}=i\hskip 0.85355ptN^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}_{c}\!\int\!d\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{1}\hskip 0.56917ptd\hskip 0.56917pt{\bf k}_{2}\,|\mathscr{T}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\,{\bf k}_{2}}|^{\hskip 0.56917pt2}\,\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}\!+i\hskip 0.85355pt0}\,N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{1}}N^{\hskip 0.85355ptl}_{{\bf k}_{2}}\bigl\langle\hskip 0.28436pt\mathcal{Q}^{\hskip 0.85355ptd}\hskip 0.85355pt\bigr\rangle.

(9.4)

However, it is not the end of the story. In the final step, we use Sohotsky’s formula

\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}\!+i\hskip 0.85355pt0}=\mathcal{P}\frac{1}{\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}}-\hskip 0.56917pti\hskip 0.56917pt\pi\hskip 0.85355pt\delta(\Delta\hskip 0.85355pt\omega_{\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}),

where the symbol
\mathcal{P}
denotes the principle value. The square of the amplitude modulus
|\mathscr{T}_{\;{\bf k}_{1},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}|^{\hskip 0.85355pt2}
, by virtue of property (
5.9
), is an even function relative to the exchange
{\bf k}_{1}\rightleftarrows{\bf k}_{2}
, whereas the function
\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}
by virtue of the definition (
4.11
) is an odd function with respect to the same exchange, that is,
\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\,{\bf k}_{1},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{2}}=-\Delta\hskip 0.56917pt\omega_{\,{\bf k}_{2},\hskip 0.85355pt{\bf k}_{1}}
. Therefore, in the integrand of (
9.4
